No public target was contacted. Inputs are authored lab fixtures; tool      results are real.                                                                                                   This is an evidence-linked demonstration, not a complete security assessment.                                                                                 I will render the verified Markdown as readable HTML and index the real workspace artifacts with SHA-256 hashes.⚙
